Implement the following plan:

# Issue #3: Eigener Repeater pro Operator (Stammdaten)

## Context
Operatoren sollen ihren "eigenen Repeater" (Heimat-Repeater) in den Stammdaten hinterlegen können. Das Feld ist optional (Freitext). Wenn befüllt, wird es in allen Auswertungen (Web + PDF) als zusätzliche Spalte in der Teilnehmer-Liste angezeigt. Wenn leer, bleibt die Spalte leer.

## Bestandsanalyse
- `operators` Tabelle: `id`, `callsign`, `name`, `qth`, `bezirk_code`, `bundesland_code` — kein `home_repeater`
- `server/src/routes/operators.ts`: PATCH nutzt dynamisches `fields`-Array (Zeile ~103), einfach erweiterbar
- `client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx`: Inline-Edit-Tabelle mit Add-Form
- Participants-Queries in 4 Dateien: `exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts` (Routes), `pdf.ts` (Service)
- Freitext statt FK auf repeaters-Tabelle — Operator kann Repeater nutzen, die nicht im System sind

## Änderungen

### 1. Schema-Migration: `server/src/db/schema.ts`
- `ALTER TABLE operators ADD COLUMN home_repeater TEXT DEFAULT NULL`
- PRAGMA table_info Check wie üblich

### 2. API: `server/src/routes/operators.ts`
- **POST** `/operators`: `home_repeater` akzeptieren und speichern
- **PATCH** `/operators/:id`: `'home_repeater'` zum `fields`-Array hinzufügen
- **GET** `/operators`: `home_repeater` wird automatisch mit SELECT * zurückgegeben

### 3. Stats-Queries: Participants mit `home_repeater`
4 Stellen anpassen — überall wo `o.callsign`, `o.name` selektiert wird, auch `o.home_repeater` hinzufügen:
- `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` — GET `/exercises/:id/stats` participants query
- `server/src/routes/reports.ts` — GET `/reports/stats` participants query
- `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` — beide participants queries (aggregated + single exercise)

### 4. PDF Service: `server/src/services/pdf.ts`
- `Participant` Interface: `home_repeater: string | null` hinzufügen
- `renderParticipants()`: Neue Spalte "REP." zwischen Callsign und QTH (oder am Ende)
- Spaltenbreiten anpassen

### 5. Client Types: `client/src/types/stats.ts`
- `Participant` Interface: `home_repeater: string | null` hinzufügen

### 6. Auswertung Web: `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` + `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`
- Neue Spalte "Repeater" in der Teilnehmer-Tabelle
- Zeigt `p.home_repeater || ''`

### 7. Stammdaten UI: `client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx`
- Neue Spalte "Repeater" in der Operatoren-Tabelle
- Inline-Edit: Text-Input für `home_repeater`
- Add-Form: Optionales `home_repeater` Feld
- `newOp` State: `home_repeater: ''` hinzufügen

### 8. CHANGELOG: `CHANGELOG-2026-02-24.md`
- Issue #3 Eintrag

## Dateien

| Datei | Änderung |
|-------|----------|
| `server/src/db/schema.ts` | ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN `home_repeater` |
| `server/src/routes/operators.ts` | POST/PATCH: home_repeater Feld |
| `server/src/routes/exercises.ts` | Stats participants: o.home_repeater |
| `server/src/routes/reports.ts` | Stats participants: o.home_repeater |
| `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` | PDF participants: o.home_repeater |
| `server/src/services/pdf.ts` | Neue Spalte in renderParticipants |
| `client/src/types/stats.ts` | Participant.home_repeater |
| `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx` | Repeater-Spalte in Teilnehmer-Tabelle |
| `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` | Repeater-Spalte in Teilnehmer-Tabelle |
| `client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx` | Repeater-Spalte + Edit/Add |
| `CHANGELOG-2026-02-24.md` | Dokumentation |

## Verifikation
1. `nix-shell -p nodejs --run "npm run build"` kompiliert sauber
2. OperatorsPage: Repeater-Feld sichtbar, editierbar, speichern funktioniert
3. Auswertung (Einzel-Übung): Teilnehmer-Liste zeigt Repeater-Spalte
4. Auswertung (Aggregiert): Teilnehmer-Liste zeigt Repeater-Spalte
5. PDF-Download: Teilnehmer mit Repeater-Spalte
6. Operatoren ohne Repeater: Spalte bleibt leer
7. Deploy mit `./deploy-production.sh`

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→import { callsignToCountryCode } from '../utils/callsignCountry.js';
     4→import { qthToBezirkCode } from '../utils/qthBezirk.js';
     5→
     6→export const operatorsRouter = Router();
     7→
     8→// Search/list operators
     9→operatorsRouter.get('/', (req, res) => {
    10→  const db = getDb();
    11→  const q = req.query.q as string;
    12→  if (q) {
    13→    const pattern = `%${q.toUpperCase()}%`;
    14→    const operators = db.prepare(`
    15→      SELECT o.*, bl.name as bundesland_name,
    16→        (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports sr WHERE sr.operator_id = o.id AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL) as total_reports
    17→      FROM operators o
    18→      LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    19→      WHERE o.callsign LIKE ? OR UPPER(o.name) LIKE ? OR UPPER(o.qth) LIKE ?
    20→      ORDER BY o.callsign LIMIT 50
    21→    `).all(pattern, pattern, pattern);
    22→    res.json(operators);
    23→  } else {
    24→    const limit = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(req.query.limit as string) || 100, 1), 1000);
    25→    const offset = Math.max(parseInt(req.query.offset as string) || 0, 0);
    26→    const operators = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators ORDER BY callsign LIMIT ? OFFSET ?').all(limit, offset);
    27→    const total = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM operators').get() as any).c;
    28→    res.json({ operators, total });
    29→  }
    30→});
    31→
    32→// Get single operator with history
    33→operatorsRouter.get('/:id', (req, res) => {
    34→  const db = getDb();
    35→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    36→  if (!operator) {
    37→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Operator nicht gefunden' });
    38→    return;
    39→  }
    40→  const history = db.prepare(`
    41→    SELECT e.date, e.id as exercise_id,
    42→      GROUP_CONCAT(r.short_name || ': ' ||
    43→        CASE WHEN sr.is_op_marker = 1 THEN 'OP'
    44→        ELSE COALESCE(sr.readability || '/' || sr.strength || COALESCE(sr.db_over_s9, ''), '—')
    45→        END, ' | ') as reports
    46→    FROM signal_reports sr
    47→    JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id
    48→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    49→    WHERE sr.operator_id = ?
    50→    GROUP BY e.id
    51→    ORDER BY e.date DESC
    52→  `).all(req.params.id);
    53→  res.json({ ...operator, history });
    54→});
    55→
    56→// Quick-add operator
    57→operatorsRouter.post('/', (req, res) => {
    58→  const db = getDb();
    59→  const { callsign, name, qth, bezirk_code, bundesland_code } = req.body;
    60→  if (!callsign) {
    61→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Rufzeichen erforderlich' });
    62→    return;
    63→  }
    64→  try {
    65→    const derivedBl = bundesland_code || callsignToCountryCode(callsign) || null;
    66→    const derivedBz = bezirk_code || (qth ? qthToBezirkCode(db, qth, derivedBl) : null);
    67→    const result = db.prepare(`
    68→      INSERT INTO operators (callsign, name, qth, bezirk_code, bundesland_code)
    69→      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    70→    `).run(callsign.toUpperCase(), name || null, qth || null, derivedBz, derivedBl);
    71→    const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(result.lastInsertRowid);
    72→    res.status(201).json(operator);
    73→  } catch (e: any) {
    74→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE')) {
    75→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Rufzeichen existiert bereits' });
    76→    } else {
    77→      throw e;
    78→    }
    79→  }
    80→});
    81→
    82→// Delete operator
    83→operatorsRouter.delete('/:id', (req, res) => {
    84→  const db = getDb();
    85→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
    86→  if (!operator) {
    87→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Operator nicht gefunden' });
    88→    return;
    89→  }
    90→  const refs = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE operator_id = ?').get(req.params.id) as any).c;
    91→  if (refs > 0) {
    92→    res.status(409).json({ error: `Operator hat ${refs} Rapporte und kann nicht gelöscht werden` });
    93→    return;
    94→  }
    95→  db.prepare('DELETE FROM exercise_attendance WHERE operator_id = ?').run(req.params.id);
    96→  db.prepare('DELETE FROM operators WHERE id = ?').run(req.params.id);
    97→  res.json({ ok: true });
    98→});
    99→
   100→// Update operator
   101→operatorsRouter.patch('/:id', (req, res) => {
   102→  const db = getDb();
   103→  const fields = ['callsign', 'name', 'qth', 'bezirk_code', 'bundesland_code', 'is_bos_arsa', 'is_arena', 'is_portable', 'is_exercise_support', 'gender', 'email', 'equipment', 'antenna', 'has_emergency_power', 'has_lighthouse_readiness', 'latitude', 'longitude'];
   104→  const updates: string[] = [];
   105→  const params: any[] = [];
   106→  for (const f of fields) {
   107→    if (req.body[f] !== undefined) {
   108→      updates.push(`${f} = ?`);
   109→      params.push(f === 'callsign' ? req.body[f].toUpperCase() : req.body[f]);
   110→    }
   111→  }
   112→  // Auto-derive bezirk from QTH when QTH changes and bezirk_code not explicitly set
   113→  if (req.body.qth !== undefined && req.body.bezirk_code === undefined) {
   114→    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT bundesland_code, bezirk_code FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id) as any;
   115→    if (existing && !existing.bezirk_code) {
   116→      const bl = req.body.bundesland_code ?? existing.bundesland_code;
   117→      const derived = qthToBezirkCode(db, req.body.qth, bl);
   118→      if (derived) {
   119→        updates.push('bezirk_code = ?');
   120→        params.push(derived);
   121→      }
   122→    }
   123→  }
   124→  if (updates.length === 0) {
   125→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'Keine Änderungen' });
   126→    return;
   127→  }
   128→  updates.push("updated_at = datetime('now')");
   129→  params.push(req.params.id);
   130→  db.prepare(`UPDATE operators SET ${updates.join(', ')} WHERE id = ?`).run(...params);
   131→  const operator = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM operators WHERE id = ?').get(req.params.id);
   132→  res.json(operator);
   133→});

     7→
     8→// List all exercises with summary stats
     9→exercisesRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
    10→  const db = getDb();
    11→  const exercises = db.prepare(`
    12→    SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.notes, e.oe_link_enabled, e.exercise_type, e.simplex_bezirke, e.simplex_bl_codes,
    13→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    14→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    15→    FROM exercises e ORDER BY e.date ASC
    16→  `).all();
    17→  res.json(exercises);

(oe_link_enabled ? 1 : 0) : 1, exercise_type || 'Krisenkommunikationsübung', simplex_bezirke ? 1 : 0, simplex_bl_codes || null);
    30→  const exercise = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM exercises WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    31→  res.status(201).json(exercise);

    34→// Get full exercise with reports

    39→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Übung nicht gefunden' });
    40→    return;
    41→  }
    42→
    43→  const repeaters = db.prepare(`
    44→    SELECT er.*, r.short_name, r.site_name, r.band, r.callsign as repeater_callsign,
    45→      r.frequency_mhz, r.offset_mhz, r.ctcss_hz, r.burst_hz, r.type, r.is_linked,
    46→      r.bundesland_code, bl.name as bundesland_name
    47→    FROM exercise_repeaters er
    48→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = er.repeater_id
    49→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = r.bundesland_code
    50→    WHERE er.exercise_id = ?
    51→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, r.sort_order
    52→  `).all(req.params.id);
    53→
    54→  const reports = db.prepare(`
    55→    SELECT sr.*, o.callsign, o.name as operator_name, o.qth as operator_qth, sr.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
    56→      r.short_name as repeater_name, r.bundesland_code as repeater_bundesland_code, r.is_linked as repeater_is_linked,
    57→      ep.abbreviation as einstiegspunkt_abbr, ep.site_name as einstiegspunkt_name
    58→    FROM signal_reports sr
    59→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
    60→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    61→    LEFT JOIN einstiegspunkte ep ON ep.id = sr.einstiegspunkt_id
    62→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ?
    63→    ORDER BY r.bundesland_code, o.callsign
    64→  `).all(req.params.id);
    65→
    66→  const attendance = db.prepare(`

    68→    FROM exercise_attendance ea
    69→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = ea.operator_id
    70→    WHERE ea.exercise_id = ?
    71→    ORDER BY o.callsign
    72→  `).all(req.params.id);

   118→// Get exercise stats
   119→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/stats', (req, res) => {
   120→  const db = getDb();
   121→  const eid = req.params.id;
   122→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   123→  const totalReports = (db.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = ? AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL').get(eid) as any)?.c || 0;
   124→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(`
   125→    SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
   126→    FROM signal_reports sr
   127→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   128→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   129→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
   130→    ORDER BY r.sort_order
   131→  `).all(eid);
   132→
   133→  // Bezirk breakdown: participants + reports per bezirk, grouped by bundesland
   134→  const bezirkStats = db.prepare(`
   135→    SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code, bl.sort_order as bl_sort,
   136→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
   137→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   138→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   139→    FROM signal_reports sr
   140→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
   141→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
   142→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   143→    GROUP BY bz.code
   144→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code
   145→  `).all(eid);
   146→
   147→  // Bundesland breakdown
   148→  const blStats = db.prepare(`
   149→    SELECT
   150→      COALESCE(bl.name, 'Sonstige') as bundesland,
   151→      o.bundesland_code,
   152→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
   153→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   154→    FROM signal_reports sr
   155→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   156→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   157→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   158→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
   159→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code
   160→  `).all(eid);
   161→
   162→  // Participant list: all operators with their report counts
   163→  const participants = db.prepare(`
   164→    SELECT o.callsign, o.name, o.bezirk_code, o.bundesland_code,
   165→      bl.name as bundesland_name,
   166→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as report_count,
   167→      GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix) as suffixes
   168→    FROM signal_reports sr
   169→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   170→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
   171→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   172→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id
   173→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, o.bezirk_code, o.callsign
   174→  `).all(eid);
   175→
   176→  res.json({ totalParticipants, totalReports, perRepeater, bezirkStats, blStats, participants });
   177→});

   260→
   261→      db.prepare('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO exercise_attendance (exercise_id, operator_id, entered_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?)')

   356→  } catch (e: any) {
   357→    if (e.message?.includes('UNIQUE') || e.message?.includes('PRIMARY')) {
   358→      res.status(409).json({ error: 'Teilnehmer bereits eingetragen' });
   359→    } else {
   360→      throw e;
   361→    }
   362→  }
   363→});
   364→
   365→// Nebenstationen report (legacy, data now included in /stats)
   366→exercisesRouter.get('/:id/nebenstationen', (req, res) => {
   367→  const db = getDb();
   368→  const data = db.prepare(`
   369→    SELECT bl.name as bundesland, bl.code as bundesland_code,
   370→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
   371→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as count,
   372→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
   373→    FROM signal_reports sr
   374→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
   375→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
   376→    WHERE sr.exercise_id = ? AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   377→    GROUP BY bz.code
   378→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code
   379→  `).all(req.params.id);
   380→  res.json(data);
   381→});
   382→

     1→import { Router } from 'express';
     2→import { getDb } from '../db/database.js';
     3→
     4→export const reportsRouter = Router();
     5→
     6→// Aggregated stats across a date range
     7→reportsRouter.get('/stats', (req, res) => {
     8→  const { from, to, types } = req.query;
     9→  if (!from || !to) {
    10→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
    11→    return;
    12→  }
    13→
    14→  const db = getDb();
    15→
    16→  // Fetch exercises in date range, optionally filtered by type
    17→  let typeFilter = '';
    18→  const queryParams: any[] = [from, to];
    19→  if (types && typeof types === 'string' && types.trim()) {
    20→    const typeList = types.split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    21→    if (typeList.length > 0) {
    22→      typeFilter = ` AND e.exercise_type IN (${typeList.map(() => '?').join(',')})`;
    23→      queryParams.push(...typeList);
    24→    }
    25→  }
    26→
    27→  const exercises = db.prepare(
    28→    `SELECT e.id, e.date, e.name, e.oe_link_enabled, e.exercise_type,
    29→      (SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0) as participant_count,
    30→      (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id = e.id AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL) as report_count
    31→    FROM exercises e
    32→    WHERE e.date >= ? AND e.date <= ?${typeFilter}
    33→    ORDER BY e.date ASC`
    34→  ).all(...queryParams) as any[];
    35→
    36→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
    37→    res.json({
    38→      exercises: [],
    39→      stats: { totalParticipants: 0, totalReports: 0, perRepeater: [], bezirkStats: [], blStats: [], participants: [] },
    40→    });
    41→    return;

    43→
    44→  const ids = exercises.map((e: any) => e.id);
    45→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
    46→
    47→  const totalParticipants = (db.prepare(
    48→    `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT operator_id) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0`
    49→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    50→
    51→  const totalReports = (db.prepare(
    52→    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM signal_reports WHERE exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND is_op_marker = 0 AND readability IS NOT NULL`
    53→  ).get(...ids) as any)?.c || 0;
    54→
    55→  const perRepeater = db.prepare(
    56→    `SELECT r.short_name, COUNT(*) as count
    57→    FROM signal_reports sr
    58→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
    59→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0 AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
    60→    GROUP BY sr.repeater_id
    61→    ORDER BY count DESC`
    62→  ).all(...ids);
    63→

    66→      bz.code as bezirk_code, bz.name as bezirk_name, bz.is_capital,
    67→      COUNT(DISTINCT sr.operator_id) as participants,
    68→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports
    69→    FROM signal_reports sr
    70→    JOIN bezirke bz ON bz.code = sr.bezirk_code
    71→    JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = bz.bundesland_code
    72→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    73→    GROUP BY bz.code
    74→    ORDER BY bl.sort_order, bz.code`
    75→  ).all(...ids);
    76→

    80→      o.bundesland_code,

    82→      COUNT(CASE WHEN sr.readability IS NOT NULL THEN 1 END) as reports

    85→    LEFT JOIN bundeslaender bl ON bl.code = o.bundesland_code
    86→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
    87→    GROUP BY o.bundesland_code
    88→    ORDER BY o.bundesland_code`

    99→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders}) AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   100→    GROUP BY sr.operator_id

   102→  ).all(...ids);

   110→// ADIF export across a date range
   111→reportsRouter.get('/adif', (req, res) => {
   112→  const { from, to } = req.query;
   113→  if (!from || !to) {
   114→    res.status(400).json({ error: 'from und to Parameter erforderlich (YYYY-MM-DD)' });
   115→    return;
   116→  }
   117→
   118→  const db = getDb();
   119→
   120→  const exercises = db.prepare(
   121→    `SELECT id, date FROM exercises WHERE date >= ? AND date <= ? ORDER BY date ASC`
   122→  ).all(from, to) as { id: string; date: string }[];
   123→
   124→  if (exercises.length === 0) {
   125→    res.status(404).json({ error: 'Keine Übungen im gewählten Zeitraum' });
   126→    return;
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  const ids = exercises.map(e => e.id);
   130→  const placeholders = ids.map(() => '?').join(',');
   131→
   132→  const reports = db.prepare(
   133→    `SELECT sr.readability, sr.strength, sr.db_over_s9, sr.notes, sr.created_at,
   134→      o.callsign, o.name as op_name, o.qth,
   135→      r.short_name, r.frequency_mhz, r.band, r.type,
   136→      e.date as exercise_date
   137→    FROM signal_reports sr
   138→    JOIN operators o ON o.id = sr.operator_id
   139→    JOIN repeaters r ON r.id = sr.repeater_id
   140→    JOIN exercises e ON e.id = sr.exercise_id
   141→    WHERE sr.exercise_id IN (${placeholders})
   142→      AND sr.is_op_marker = 0
   143→      AND sr.readability IS NOT NULL
   144→    ORDER BY e.date, sr.created_at`
   145→  ).all(...ids) as any[];
   146→
   147→  // Build ADIF
   148→  const adifField = (name: string, value: string) => {
   149→    if (!value) return '';
   150→    return `<${name}:${value.length}>${value} `;
   151→  };
   152→
   153→  let adif = '';
   154→
   155→  // Header
   156→  adif += 'ADIF Export from BOS-ARSA Log\n';
   157→  adif += `https://bosarsalog.oeradio.at/\n`;
   158→  adif += `Date range: ${from} to ${to}\n`;
   159→  adif += `QSO count: ${reports.length}\n`;
   160→  adif += '\n';
   161→  adif += adifField('ADIF_VER', '3.1.4');
   162→  adif += adifField('PROGRAMID', 'BOS-ARSA Log');
   163→  adif += adifField('PROGRAMVERSION', '1.0.5');
   164→  adif += '\n<EOH>\n\n';
   165→
   166→  // Records
   167→  for (const r of reports) {
   168→    const qsoDate = r.exercise_date.replace(/-/g, '');
   169→
   170→    // Extract time from created_at (format: "2026-02-16 14:30:00")
   171→    let timeOn = '0900'; // default
   172→    if (r.created_at) {
   173→      const timePart = r.created_at.split(' ')[1];
   174→      if (timePart) {
   175→        timeOn = timePart.replace(/:/g, '').slice(0, 4);
   176→      }
   177→    }
   178→
   179→    // RST: combine readability + strength + optional dB
   180→    const rst = `${r.readability}${r.strength}`;
   181→
   182→    // Frequency in MHz as string
   183→    const freq = r.frequency_mhz ? String(r.frequency_mhz) : '';
   184→
   185→    // Band mapping
   186→    let band = r.band || '';
   187→    if (!band && r.frequency_mhz) {
   188→      if (r.frequency_mhz >= 144 && r.frequency_mhz < 148) band = '2m';
   189→      else if (r.frequency_mhz >= 430 && r.frequency_mhz < 440) band = '70cm';
   190→      else if (r.frequency_mhz >= 1240 && r.frequency_mhz < 1300) band = '23cm';
   191→    }
   192→
   193→    adif += adifField('CALL', r.callsign);
   194→    adif += adifField('QSO_DATE', qsoDate);
   195→    adif += adifField('TIME_ON', timeOn);
   196→    adif += adifField('MODE', 'FM');
   197→    if (band) adif += adifField('BAND', band);
   198→    if (freq) adif += adifField('FREQ', freq);
   199→    adif += adifField('RST_SENT', rst);
   200→    adif += adifField('RST_RCVD', rst);
   201→    if (r.op_name) adif += adifField('NAME', r.op_name);
   202→    if (r.qth) adif += adifField('QTH', r.qth);
   203→    if (r.notes) adif += adifField('COMMENT', r.notes);
   204→    adif += '<EOR>\n';
   205→  }
   206→
   207→  const filename = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   208→  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8');
   209→  res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
   210→  res.send(adif);
   211→});
   212→

   214→reportsRouter.get('/', (_req, res) => {
   215→  res.json({ message: 'Use /api/v1/reports/stats?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD' });
   216→});
   217→

     1→import PDFDocument from 'pdfkit';
     2→
     3→// ── Color constants ──────────────────────────────────────
     4→const NAVY = '#1e3a5f';

    21→// ── Interfaces ───────────────────────────────────────────
    22→interface PerRepeater { short_name: string; count: number }
    23→interface BlStat { bundesland: string; bundesland_code: string; participants: number; reports: number }
    24→interface BezirkStat {
    25→  bundesland: string; bundesland_code: string; bl_sort?: number;
    26→  bezirk_code: string; bezirk_name: string; is_capital: number;
    27→  participants: number; reports: number;
    28→}
    29→interface Participant {
    30→  callsign: string; name: string | null; bezirk_code: string | null;
    31→  bundesland_code: string | null; bundesland_name: string | null; report_count: number;
    32→  suffixes: string | null;
    33→}
    34→interface ExerciseSummary {
    35→  id: string; date: string; name: string; exercise_type?: string | null;
    36→  participant_count: number; report_count: number;
    37→}
    38→
    39→interface PdfStats {
    40→  totalParticipants: number;
    41→  totalReports: number;
    42→  perRepeater: PerRepeater[];
    43→  bezirkStats: BezirkStat[];
    44→  blStats: BlStat[];
    45→  participants: Participant[];
    46→}

    87→
    88→const BL_SHORT: Record<string, string> = {
    89→  '01': 'OE1', '02': 'OE2', '03': 'OE3', '04': 'OE4', '05': 'OE5',
    90→  '06': 'OE6', '07': 'OE7', '08': 'OE8', '09': 'OE9',
    91→};
    92→
    93→// ── Helper functions ─────────────────────────────────────
    94→
    95→function getHeatColor(value: number, max: number): string {
    96→  if (max === 0 || value === 0) return '#e5e7eb';
    97→  const t = value / max;
    98→  const r = Math.round(194 + (30 - 194) * t);
    99→  const g = Math.round(213 + (58 - 213) * t);
   100→  const b = Math.round(236 + (95 - 236) * t);
   101→  return '#' + [r, g, b].map(c => c.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
   102→}
   103→
   104→/** Return white for dark backgrounds, navy for light backgrounds */
   105→function getLabelColor(hexBg: string): string {
   106→  const r = parseInt(hexBg.slice(1, 3), 16);
   107→  const g = parseInt(hexBg.slice(3, 5), 16);
   108→  const b = parseInt(hexBg.slice(5, 7), 16);
   109→  // Relative luminance (perceived brightness)
   110→  const lum = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b);
   111→  return lum < 150 ? WHITE : NAVY;
   112→}
   113→
   114→function formatDateDE(d: string): string {
   115→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
   116→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
   117→}
   118→
   119→function ensureSpace(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, needed: number) {
   120→  const bottom = doc.page.height - doc.page.margins.bottom;
   121→  if (doc.y + needed > bottom) {
   122→    doc.addPage();
   123→  }
   124→}
   125→
   126→/** Write text at fixed position without moving doc.y */
   127→function textAt(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, str: string, x: number, y: number, opts?: PDFKit.Mixins.TextOptions) {
   128→  doc.text(str, x, y, { lineBreak: false, ...opts });
   129→}
   130→
   131→// ── Render functions ─────────────────────────────────────
   132→
   133→function renderHeader(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, title: string, subtitle: string) {
   134→  const pageW = doc.page.width;
   135→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   136→  const barW = pageW - m.left - m.right;
   137→
   138→  doc.save();
   139→  doc.rect(m.left, m.top, barW, 50).fill(NAVY);
   140→  doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(WHITE);
   141→  textAt(doc, 'BOS-ARSA Log', m.left + 15, m.top + 10);
   142→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor('#93c5fd');
   143→  textAt(doc, 'Im Sinne der Sicherheit', m.left + 15, m.top + 32);
   144→  doc.restore();
   145→
   146→  doc.y = m.top + 60;
   147→  doc.fontSize(14).fillColor(NAVY);
   148→  textAt(doc, title, m.left, doc.y);
   149→  doc.y += 18;
   150→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   151→  textAt(doc, subtitle, m.left, doc.y);
   152→  doc.y += 16;
   153→}
   154→
   155→function renderSummaryCards(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, stats: PdfStats, exerciseCount?: number) {
   156→  ensureSpace(doc, 50);
   157→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   158→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   159→  const cols = exerciseCount !== undefined ? 4 : 2;
   160→  const cardW = usable / cols - 8;
   161→  const startY = doc.y;
   162→
   163→  const cards: { value: string | number; label: string }[] = [
   164→    { value: stats.totalParticipants, label: 'Teilnehmer' },
   165→    { value: stats.totalReports, label: 'Rapporte' },
   166→  ];
   167→  if (exerciseCount !== undefined) {
   168→    cards.push({ value: exerciseCount, label: 'Übungen' });
   169→    const avg = exerciseCount > 0 ? Math.round(stats.totalParticipants / exerciseCount * 10) / 10 : 0;
   170→    cards.push({ value: avg, label: 'Ø Teiln./Übung' });
   171→  }
   172→
   173→  cards.forEach((c, i) => {
   174→    const x = m.left + i * (cardW + 8);
   175→    doc.save();
   176→    doc.roundedRect(x, startY, cardW, 40, 4).fill(GRAY_50);
   177→    doc.fontSize(18).fillColor(NAVY);
   178→    textAt(doc, String(c.value), x, startY + 5, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   179→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   180→    textAt(doc, c.label, x, startY + 27, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   181→    doc.restore();
   182→  });
   183→  doc.y = startY + 48;
   184→}
   185→
   186→function renderPerRepeater(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, perRepeater: PerRepeater[]) {
   187→  if (perRepeater.length === 0) return;
   188→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   189→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   190→  const gap = 6;
   191→  const maxCols = 6;
   192→  const cols = Math.min(perRepeater.length, maxCols);
   193→  const cardW = usable / cols - gap;
   194→  const rowH = 28;
   195→  const rows = Math.ceil(perRepeater.length / maxCols);
   196→  ensureSpace(doc, rows * (rowH + gap) + 4);
   197→
   198→  const startY = doc.y;
   199→  perRepeater.forEach((r, i) => {
   200→    const col = i % maxCols;
   201→    const row = Math.floor(i / maxCols);
   202→    const x = m.left + col * (cardW + gap);
   203→    const y = startY + row * (rowH + gap);
   204→    doc.save();
   205→    doc.roundedRect(x, y, cardW, rowH, 3).fill(GRAY_50);
   206→    doc.fontSize(12).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   207→    textAt(doc, String(r.count), x, y + 2, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   208→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   209→    textAt(doc, r.short_name, x, y + 17, { width: cardW, align: 'center' });
   210→    doc.restore();
   211→  });
   212→  doc.y = startY + rows * (rowH + gap) + 4;
   213→}
   214→
   215→function renderExerciseTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, exercises: ExerciseSummary[]) {
   216→  if (exercises.length === 0) return;
   217→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   218→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   219→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   220→
   221→  // Section header
   222→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   223→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   224→  textAt(doc, `Übungen (${exercises.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   225→  doc.y += 20;
   226→
   227→  // Column positions
   228→  const colDate = m.left + 5;
   229→  const colName = m.left + 80;
   230→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   231→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   232→
   233→  // Column headers
   234→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   235→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   236→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   237→  textAt(doc, 'DATUM', colDate, hy);
   238→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   239→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   240→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   241→  doc.y += 16;
   242→
   243→  exercises.forEach((ex, i) => {
   244→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   245→    const rowY = doc.y;
   246→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   247→      doc.save();
   248→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 12).fill(GRAY_50);
   249→      doc.restore();
   250→    }
   251→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   252→    textAt(doc, formatDateDE(ex.date), colDate, rowY + 2);
   253→    textAt(doc, ex.name || '', colName, rowY + 2, { width: colPart - colName - 10 });
   254→    textAt(doc, String(ex.participant_count), colPart, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   255→    textAt(doc, String(ex.report_count), colRap, rowY + 2, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   256→    doc.y = rowY + 13;
   257→  });
   258→  doc.y += 4;
   259→}
   260→
   261→function renderAustriaMap(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[]) {
   262→  if (blStats.length === 0) return;
   263→  ensureSpace(doc, 200);
   264→
   265→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   266→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   267→  const mapW = Math.min(usable, 400);
   268→  const mapH = mapW * 0.5;
   269→  const offsetX = m.left + (usable - mapW) / 2;
   270→
   271→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   272→  textAt(doc, 'Teilnehmer nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);
   273→  doc.y += 14;
   274→  const mapStartY = doc.y;
   275→
   276→  const dataMap = new Map(blStats.map(bl => [bl.bundesland_code, bl]));
   277→  const maxP = Math.max(...blStats.map(bl => bl.participants), 1);
   278→
   279→  const svgW = 620;
   280→  const svgH = 300;
   281→  const scale = Math.min(mapW / svgW, mapH / svgH);
   282→
   283→  for (const [code, pathData] of Object.entries(BL_PATHS)) {
   284→    const bl = dataMap.get(code);
   285→    const participants = bl?.participants ?? 0;
   286→    const fillColor = getHeatColor(participants, maxP);
   287→
   288→    doc.save();
   289→    doc.translate(offsetX, mapStartY);
   290→    doc.scale(scale);
   291→    doc.path(pathData.d).lineWidth(2 / scale).fillAndStroke(fillColor, WHITE);
   292→    doc.restore();
   293→
   294→    // Label — contrast-aware color based on heatmap background
   295→    const lx = offsetX + pathData.labelX * scale;
   296→    const ly = mapStartY + pathData.labelY * scale;
   297→    const labelColor = getLabelColor(fillColor);
   298→    doc.save();
   299→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(labelColor);
   300→    textAt(doc, BL_SHORT[code] || code, lx - 15, ly - 6, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   301→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(labelColor);
   302→    textAt(doc, String(participants), lx - 15, ly + 1, { width: 30, align: 'center' });
   303→    doc.restore();
   304→  }
   305→
   306→  doc.y = mapStartY + mapH + 10;
   307→}
   308→
   309→function renderBarChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; participants: number; reports: number }[]) {
   310→  if (chartData.length === 0) return;
   311→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   312→
   313→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   314→  const chartW = Math.min(doc.page.width - m.left - m.right, 500);
   315→  const chartH = 140;
   316→  const startX = m.left;
   317→
   318→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   319→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen & Rapporte nach Bundesland', m.left, doc.y);
   320→  doc.y += 15;
   321→  const startY = doc.y;
   322→
   323→  const maxVal = Math.max(...chartData.map(d => Math.max(d.participants, d.reports)), 1);
   324→  const barAreaW = chartW - 30;
   325→  const groupW = barAreaW / chartData.length;
   326→  const barW = Math.min(groupW * 0.35, 20);
   327→
   328→  // Y-axis gridlines
   329→  for (let i = 0; i <= 4; i++) {
   330→    const y = startY + chartH - (chartH * i / 4);
   331→    const val = Math.round(maxVal * i / 4);
   332→    doc.save();
   333→    doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(startX + 25, y).lineTo(startX + chartW, y).stroke();
   334→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   335→    textAt(doc, String(val), startX, y - 3, { width: 22, align: 'right' });
   336→    doc.restore();
   337→  }
   338→
   339→  // Bars
   340→  chartData.forEach((d, i) => {
   341→    const groupX = startX + 30 + i * groupW;
   342→    const pH = (d.participants / maxVal) * chartH;
   343→    doc.rect(groupX, startY + chartH - pH, barW, pH).fill(NAVY);
   344→    const rH = (d.reports / maxVal) * chartH;
   345→    doc.rect(groupX + barW + 2, startY + chartH - rH, barW, rH).fill(RED);
   346→    doc.save();
   347→    doc.fontSize(4).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   348→    textAt(doc, d.label, groupX - 2, startY + chartH + 3, { width: groupW });
   349→    doc.restore();
   350→  });
   351→
   352→  // Legend
   353→  const legendY = startY + chartH + 14;
   354→  doc.rect(startX + 30, legendY, 8, 6).fill(NAVY);
   355→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   356→  textAt(doc, 'Stationen', startX + 42, legendY);
   357→  doc.rect(startX + 90, legendY, 8, 6).fill(RED);
   358→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   359→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte', startX + 102, legendY);
   360→  doc.y = legendY + 14;
   361→}
   362→
   363→function renderPieChart(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, chartData: { label: string; value: number }[]) {
   364→  const filtered = chartData.filter(d => d.value > 0);
   365→  if (filtered.length === 0) return;
   366→  ensureSpace(doc, 180);
   367→
   368→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   369→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   370→
   371→  doc.fontSize(9).fillColor(NAVY);
   372→  textAt(doc, 'Rapporte Verteilung', m.left, doc.y);
   373→  doc.y += 12;
   374→
   375→  const cx = m.left + 100;
   376→  const cy = doc.y + 70;
   377→  const r = 60;
   378→  const total = filtered.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0);
   379→  let startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;
   380→
   381→  filtered.forEach((d, i) => {
   382→    const sliceAngle = (d.value / total) * 2 * Math.PI;
   383→    const endAngle = startAngle + sliceAngle;
   384→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   385→
   386→    const x1 = cx + r * Math.cos(startAngle);
   387→    const y1 = cy + r * Math.sin(startAngle);
   388→    const x2 = cx + r * Math.cos(endAngle);
   389→    const y2 = cy + r * Math.sin(endAngle);
   390→    const largeArc = sliceAngle > Math.PI ? 1 : 0;
   391→
   392→    doc.path(`M ${cx} ${cy} L ${x1} ${y1} A ${r} ${r} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${x2} ${y2} Z`).fill(color);
   393→    startAngle = endAngle;
   394→  });
   395→
   396→  // Legend (limit to available space)
   397→  const legendX = cx + r + 30;
   398→  const maxLegendItems = Math.floor(((doc.page.height - m.bottom) - (cy - r)) / 12);
   399→  const legendItems = filtered.slice(0, maxLegendItems);
   400→  legendItems.forEach((d, i) => {
   401→    const ly = cy - r + i * 12;
   402→    const color = PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length];
   403→    doc.rect(legendX, ly, 8, 8).fill(color);
   404→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   405→    textAt(doc, `${d.label} (${d.value})`, legendX + 12, ly + 1, { width: usable - legendX + m.left - 12 });
   406→  });
   407→  if (filtered.length > maxLegendItems) {
   408→    const overflowY = cy - r + maxLegendItems * 12;
   409→    doc.fontSize(5).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   410→    textAt(doc, `… +${filtered.length - maxLegendItems} weitere`, legendX, overflowY);
   411→  }
   412→
   413→  doc.y = cy + r + 15;
   414→}
   415→
   416→function renderBezirkTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, bezirkStats: BezirkStat[], stats: PdfStats) {
   417→  if (bezirkStats.length === 0) return;
   418→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   419→
   420→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   421→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   422→  const colName = m.left + 5;
   423→  const colPart = m.left + usable - 90;
   424→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 40;
   425→
   426→  // Section header
   427→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   428→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   429→  textAt(doc, 'Nebenstationen nach Bezirk', m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   430→  doc.y += 20;
   431→
   432→  // Column headers
   433→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   434→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   435→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   436→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND / BEZIRK', colName, hy);
   437→  textAt(doc, 'TEILN.', colPart, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   438→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   439→  doc.y += 16;
   440→
   441→  // Group by Bundesland
   442→  const byBl: Record<string, { name: string; bezirke: BezirkStat[]; totalP: number; totalR: number }> = {};
   443→  for (const bz of bezirkStats) {
   444→    if (!byBl[bz.bundesland_code]) {
   445→      byBl[bz.bundesland_code] = { name: bz.bundesland, bezirke: [], totalP: 0, totalR: 0 };
   446→    }
   447→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].bezirke.push(bz);
   448→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalP += bz.participants;
   449→    byBl[bz.bundesland_code].totalR += bz.reports;
   450→  }
   451→
   452→  for (const [, bl] of Object.entries(byBl)) {
   453→    ensureSpace(doc, 14);
   454→    const rowY = doc.y;
   455→    doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 13).fill(GRAY_100);
   456→    doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   457→    textAt(doc, bl.name, colName, rowY + 3);
   458→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalP), colPart, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   459→    textAt(doc, String(bl.totalR), colRap, rowY + 3, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   460→    doc.y = rowY + 14;
   461→
   462→    bl.bezirke.forEach(bz => {
   463→      ensureSpace(doc, 12);
   464→      const ry = doc.y;
   465→      doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   466→      textAt(doc, `    ${bz.bezirk_code}  ${bz.bezirk_name}`, colName, ry + 1);
   467→      textAt(doc, String(bz.participants), colPart, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   468→      textAt(doc, String(bz.reports), colRap, ry + 1, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   469→      doc.y = ry + 11;
   470→    });
   471→  }
   472→
   473→  // Gesamt
   474→  ensureSpace(doc, 18);
   475→  const gy = doc.y;
   476→  doc.rect(m.left, gy, usable, 16).fill(NAVY);
   477→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(WHITE);
   478→  textAt(doc, 'Gesamt', colName, gy + 4);
   479→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalParticipants), colPart, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   480→  textAt(doc, String(stats.totalReports), colRap, gy + 4, { width: 40, align: 'right' });
   481→  doc.y = gy + 20;
   482→}
   483→
   484→function renderBlTable(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, blStats: BlStat[], stats: PdfStats) {

   493→
   494→  // Section header

   499→
   500→  // Column headers

   524→  // Gesamt

   532→  doc.y = gy + 20;

   534→
   535→function renderParticipants(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument, participants: Participant[]) {
   536→  if (participants.length === 0) return;
   537→  ensureSpace(doc, 60);
   538→
   539→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   540→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   541→
   542→  // Section header
   543→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 18).fill(NAVY);
   544→  doc.fontSize(8).fillColor(WHITE);
   545→  textAt(doc, `Teilnehmer-Liste (${participants.length})`, m.left + 8, doc.y + 4);
   546→  doc.y += 20;
   547→
   548→  // Column positions
   549→  const colNum = m.left + 3;
   550→  const colCall = m.left + 25;
   551→  const colName = m.left + 90;
   552→  const colBz = m.left + usable - 135;
   553→  const colBl = m.left + usable - 90;
   554→  const colRap = m.left + usable - 35;
   555→
   556→  // Column headers
   557→  doc.rect(m.left, doc.y, usable, 14).fill(GRAY_100);
   558→  doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   559→  const hy = doc.y + 4;
   560→  textAt(doc, '#', colNum, hy);
   561→  textAt(doc, 'RUFZEICHEN', colCall, hy);
   562→  textAt(doc, 'NAME', colName, hy);
   563→  textAt(doc, 'BEZ.', colBz, hy);
   564→  textAt(doc, 'BUNDESLAND', colBl, hy);
   565→  textAt(doc, 'RAPP.', colRap, hy, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   566→  doc.y += 16;
   567→
   568→  participants.forEach((p, i) => {
   569→    ensureSpace(doc, 11);
   570→    const rowY = doc.y;
   571→    if (i % 2 === 0) {
   572→      doc.save();
   573→      doc.rect(m.left, rowY, usable, 10).fill(GRAY_50);
   574→      doc.restore();
   575→    }
   576→    const ty = rowY + 2;
   577→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   578→    textAt(doc, String(i + 1), colNum, ty);
   579→    const suffixStr = p.suffixes ? p.suffixes.split(',').filter(Boolean).map(s => s.startsWith('/') ? s : `/${s}`).join('') : '';
   580→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   581→    textAt(doc, p.callsign + suffixStr, colCall, ty);
   582→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_MED);
   583→    textAt(doc, p.name || '', colName, ty, { width: colBz - colName - 5 });
   584→    textAt(doc, p.bezirk_code || '', colBz, ty);
   585→    textAt(doc, p.bundesland_name || '', colBl, ty, { width: colRap - colBl - 5 });
   586→    doc.fontSize(6).fillColor(TEXT_DARK);
   587→    textAt(doc, String(p.report_count), colRap, ty, { width: 35, align: 'right' });
   588→    doc.y = rowY + 11;
   589→  });
   590→  doc.y += 4;
   591→}
   592→
   593→function renderFooter(doc: PDFKit.PDFDocument) {
   594→  const m = doc.page.margins;
   595→  const usable = doc.page.width - m.left - m.right;
   596→  ensureSpace(doc, 25);
   597→  doc.strokeColor(GRAY_200).lineWidth(0.5).moveTo(m.left, doc.y).lineTo(m.left + usable, doc.y).stroke();
   598→  doc.y += 8;
   599→  doc.fontSize(7).fillColor(GRAY_500);
   600→  textAt(doc, 'oeradio.at  ·  bosarsa.oeradio.at', m.left, doc.y, { width: usable, align: 'center' });
   601→}
   602→
   603→// ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────
   604→
   605→export function generateAggregatedPdf(
   606→  exercises: ExerciseSummary[],
   607→  stats: PdfStats,
   608→  from: string,
   609→  to: string,
   610→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   611→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   612→    layout: 'landscape',
   613→    size: 'A4',
   614→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   615→    autoFirstPage: true,
   616→    bufferPages: true,
   617→  });
   618→
   619→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${formatDateDE(from)} – ${formatDateDE(to)}  ·  ${exercises.length} Übung${exercises.length !== 1 ? 'en' : ''}`);
   620→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats, exercises.length);
   621→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   622→  renderExerciseTable(doc, exercises);
   623→
   624→  // Page 2: Austria map
   625→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   626→    doc.addPage();
   627→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   628→  }
   629→
   630→  // Page 3: Charts (always BL-level for readability, max 9 entries)
   631→  const chartData = stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   632→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   633→    doc.addPage();
   634→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   635→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   636→  }
   637→
   638→  // Page 4+: Bezirk/BL detail table
   639→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   640→    doc.addPage();
   641→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   642→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   643→    doc.addPage();
   644→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   645→  }
   646→
   647→  // Page 5+: Participants
   648→  doc.addPage();
   649→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   650→  renderFooter(doc);
   651→
   652→  doc.end();
   653→  return doc;
   654→}
   655→
   656→export function generateExercisePdf(
   657→  exercise: { name: string; date: string; exercise_type?: string | null },
   658→  stats: PdfStats,
   659→): PDFKit.PDFDocument {
   660→  const doc = new PDFDocument({
   661→    layout: 'landscape',
   662→    size: 'A4',
   663→    margins: { top: 30, bottom: 30, left: 40, right: 40 },
   664→    autoFirstPage: true,
   665→    bufferPages: true,
   666→  });
   667→
   668→  const formattedDate = new Date(exercise.date + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('de-AT', {
   669→    weekday: 'long', day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric',
   670→  });
   671→  renderHeader(doc, 'Auswertung', `${exercise.name} — ${formattedDate}`);
   672→  renderSummaryCards(doc, stats);
   673→  renderPerRepeater(doc, stats.perRepeater);
   674→
   675→  // Page 2: Austria map
   676→  if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   677→    doc.addPage();
   678→    renderAustriaMap(doc, stats.blStats);
   679→  }
   680→
   681→  // Page 3: Charts (always BL-level for readability, max 9 entries)
   682→  const chartData = stats.blStats.map(bl => ({ label: bl.bundesland, participants: bl.participants, reports: bl.reports }));
   683→  if (chartData.length > 0) {
   684→    doc.addPage();
   685→    renderBarChart(doc, chartData);
   686→    renderPieChart(doc, chartData.map(d => ({ label: d.label, value: d.reports })));
   687→  }
   688→
   689→  // Page 4+: Bezirk/BL detail table
   690→  if (stats.bezirkStats.length > 0) {
   691→    doc.addPage();
   692→    renderBezirkTable(doc, stats.bezirkStats, stats);
   693→  } else if (stats.blStats.length > 0) {
   694→    doc.addPage();
   695→    renderBlTable(doc, stats.blStats, stats);
   696→  }
   697→
   698→  // Page 5+: Participants
   699→  doc.addPage();
   700→  renderParticipants(doc, stats.participants);
   701→  renderFooter(doc);
   702→
   703→  doc.end();
   704→  return doc;
   705→}

    62→    setExporting(true);

    38→function formatDateDE(d: string) {
    39→  const date = new Date(d + 'T00:00:00');
    40→  return date.toLocaleDateString('de-AT', { day: '2-digit', month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' });
    41→}
    42→
    43→function toISO(d: Date) {
    44→  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    45→}
    46→
    47→export default function AggregatedReportsPage() {
    48→  const now = new Date();
    49→  const year = now.getFullYear();
    50→
    51→  const [from, setFrom] = useState(() => toISO(new Date(year, 0, 1)));

    64→  const mapCardRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    65→  const pageRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    66→
    67→  const fetchStats = (f: string, t: string, types?: Set<string>) => {
    68→    setLoading(true);

    70→    apiFetch(`/api/v1/reports/stats?from=${f}&to=${t}${typesParam}`)
    71→      .then((res) => setData(res))
    72→      .catch(() => setData(null))
    73→      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    74→  };
    75→
    76→  useEffect(() => {

    91→

   649→              >

   651→              </button>
   652→              <button
   653→                onClick={async () => {

   657→                      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
   658→                    });
   659→                    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
   660→                    const blob = await res.blob();
   661→                    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
   662→                    const link = document.createElement('a');
   663→                    link.href = url;
   664→                    link.download = `BOS-ARSA_QSOs_${from}_${to}.adi`;
   665→                    link.click();
   666→                    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
   667→                  } catch {}
   668→                }}
   669→                className="bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded text-sm font-medium"
   670→              >
   671→                Download ADIF
   672→              </button>
   673→            </div>

     1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     2→import { apiFetch } from '../services/api';
     3→
     4→export default function OperatorsPage() {
     5→  const [operators, setOperators] = useState<any[]>([]);
     6→  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
     7→  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
     8→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
     9→  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<any>(null);
    10→  const [showAdd, setShowAdd] = useState(false);
    11→  const [newOp, setNewOp] = useState({ callsign: '', name: '', qth: '', bezirk_code: '', bundesland_code: '' });
    12→  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
    13→  const debounceRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
    14→
    15→  const load = async (q?: string, offset = 0) => {
    16→    setLoading(true);
    17→    try {
    18→      if (q) {
    19→        const results = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`);
    20→        setOperators(results);
    21→        setTotal(results.length);
    22→      } else {
    23→        const data = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators?limit=50&offset=${offset}`);
    24→        setOperators(data.operators);
    25→        setTotal(data.total);
    26→      }
    27→    } catch {} finally { setLoading(false); }
    28→  };
    29→
    30→  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
    31→
    32→  const handleSearch = (q: string) => {
    33→    setSearch(q);
    34→    if (debounceRef.current) clearTimeout(debounceRef.current);
    35→    debounceRef.current = setTimeout(() => load(q), 300);
    36→  };
    37→
    38→  const handleSave = async (op: any) => {
    39→    try {
    40→      const updated = await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${op.id}`, {
    41→        method: 'PATCH',
    42→        body: JSON.stringify(op),
    43→      });
    44→      setOperators(prev => prev.map(o => o.id === updated.id ? updated : o));
    45→      setEditing(null);
    46→    } catch {}
    47→  };
    48→
    49→  const handleAdd = async () => {
    50→    try {
    51→      const created = await apiFetch('/api/v1/operators', {
    52→        method: 'POST',
    53→        body: JSON.stringify(newOp),
    54→      });
    55→      setOperators(prev => [created, ...prev]);
    56→      setShowAdd(false);
    57→      setNewOp({ callsign: '', name: '', qth: '', bezirk_code: '', bundesland_code: '' });
    58→    } catch (e: any) {
    59→      alert(e.message);
    60→    }
    61→  };
    62→
    63→  const handleDelete = async (op: any) => {
    64→    if (!confirm(`Rufzeichen ${op.callsign} wirklich löschen?`)) return;
    65→    try {
    66→      await apiFetch(`/api/v1/operators/${op.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
    67→      setOperators(prev => prev.filter(o => o.id !== op.id));
    68→      setTotal(t => t - 1);
    69→    } catch (e: any) {
    70→      alert(e.message);
    71→    }
    72→  };
    73→
    74→  const handlePage = (dir: number) => {
    75→    const newPage = page + dir;
    76→    setPage(newPage);
    77→    load(undefined, newPage * 50);
    78→  };
    79→
    80→  return (
    81→    <div className="space-y-4">
    82→      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
    83→        <h1 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-[#1e3a5f]">Rufzeichen-Register</h1>

     5→### #1 — Rapport soll verschiebbar innerhalb der Bezirke sein
     6→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/1
     7→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
     8→- **Umsetzung:**

    10→  - **Drag & Drop:** Rapport-Zeilen können per Drag & Drop zwischen Bezirk-Abschnitten verschoben werden. Das Ziel wird blau hervorgehoben. Funktioniert in Frequenzen- und OE-Link-Modus.
    11→  - Dropdown funktioniert auf allen Geräten (inkl. Mobilgeräte), Drag & Drop auf Desktop.

    15→- Produktions-Backup manuell über das bestehende Backup-Skript (`backup-bosarsalog.sh`) ausgelöst und auf Synology NAS verifiziert.
    16→- Zusätzliches lokales Backup in `backups/bosarsalog-backup-2026-02-24.db` (1,6 MB, Integritätsprüfung bestanden).

    19→- Detaillierte Analyse der gemeldeten Verwirrung bei Schöckl 2m/70cm und Lachtal im Setup durchgeführt.
    20→- **Ergebnis:** Kein Datenfehler — die Filterlogik (linked → OE-Link, non-linked → Frequenzen) funktioniert korrekt wie vorgesehen.
    21→
    22→### #2 — Option Bezirkskenner in Simplex Frequenzen anzuzeigen
    23→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/2
    24→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    25→- **Umsetzung:**
    26→  - **Neuer Toggle pro Übung:** Admin-only Checkbox "Simplex Bezirke aktivieren" in der Übungs-Einrichtung (nach OE-Link-Toggle). Standardmäßig deaktiviert.
    27→  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `simplex_bezirke INTEGER DEFAULT 0` in `exercises`-Tabelle (nicht-destruktiver ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN).
    28→  - **API:** GET/POST/PATCH Exercises-Routen unterstützen `simplex_bezirke`-Feld.

    30→  - Bezirke werden aus den gewählten Bundesländern übernommen.
    31→  - **BL-Auswahl konfigurierbar:** Unterhalb des Simplex-Bezirke-Toggles erscheint ein kompaktes Bundesländer-Grid. Admin kann pro Übung auswählen, welche BLs in der Simplex-Gruppierung angezeigt werden. Beim erstmaligen Aktivieren werden alle 9 BLs vorausgewählt. Schema: neue Spalte `simplex_bl_codes TEXT` (komma-separiert, NULL = alle).
    32→  - Drag & Drop zwischen Bezirken funktioniert im gruppierten Modus.
    33→  - Nicht zugeordnete Rapporte erscheinen im "Sonstige"-Bereich.
    34→  - Toggle aus → Rapporte werden wieder flach angezeigt (Daten bleiben erhalten).

    37→### #4 — Eingabe vom OP Rufzeichen wird nicht in Echtzeit übernommen
    38→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/4
    39→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    40→- **Umsetzung:**
    41→  - **Neues WebSocket-Event `op_callsign_updated`:** Server empfängt das Event und broadcastet es an alle anderen Clients im selben Exercise-Room.
    42→  - **LandMode (Frequenzen-Modus):** Nach dem Speichern eines OP-Rufzeichens wird `op_callsign_updated` emittiert. Eingehende Events aktualisieren `opCallsigns`-State sofort.
    43→  - **BundMode (OE-Link-Modus):** Gleicher Pattern — nach API-Call wird emittiert. Eingehende Events mappen `repeater_id` zurück auf `blCode` via `blRepSelection` und aktualisieren `blOpCallsigns`.
    44→  - Cleanup: Socket-Listener werden beim Unmount/Re-Mount korrekt entfernt.

    47→---
    48→
    49→## Offene Issues
    50→
    51→### #3 — Bei den Repeatern bitte „eigener Repeater" dazufügen — Klärung
    52→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/3
    53→- Klärungsbedarf: benutzerdefinierte Repeater können bereits über Einrichtung hinzugefügt werden. Zu klären, ob weitere Funktionalität gewünscht.
    54→
    55→### #5 — Eventuell mehrere OPs bei Simplex
    56→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/5
    57→- **Status:** Geschlossen — bereits möglich
    58→- Mehrere OPs können kommagetrennt im OP-Feld eingetragen werden, z.B. `OE1OP1, OE2OP2`. Das Feld ist ein Freitext-Input ohne Beschränkung auf ein einzelnes Rufzeichen.
    59→
    60→### #8 — Download Auswertung komplett — Server-seitiges PDF
    61→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/8
    62→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    63→- **Problem:** "Download Auswertung komplett" nutzte `html-to-image` (`toPng()`), das den gesamten DOM klonte — inkl. SVG-Karte, Chart.js-Diagramme, große Tabellen. Bei umfangreichen Auswertungen crashte der Browser (500MB+ RAM, Main Thread blockiert).
    64→- **Lösung:** PDF-Generierung auf dem Server mit PDFKit (pure JS, kein Chromium nötig).
    65→- **Umsetzung:**
    66→  - **Neue Datei `server/src/services/pdf.ts`:** PDF-Generator mit Render-Funktionen: Header (Navy-Leiste mit BOS-ARSA Branding), Summary-Cards, Per-Repeater-Grid, Übungstabelle, Austria-Karte (SVG-Pfade direkt via PDFKit gezeichnet mit Heatmap-Farben), Balkendiagramm (Rechtecke), Tortendiagramm (Kreissegmente), Bezirk/BL-Tabelle, Teilnehmer-Liste, Footer.
    67→  - **Neue Datei `server/src/routes/pdf.ts`:** Zwei Endpunkte: `GET /api/v1/pdf/reports?from=...&to=...&types=...` (aggregiert) und `GET /api/v1/pdf/exercises/:id` (Einzel-Übung).
    68→  - **Client:** `downloadFullPage()` in `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx` und `ReportsPage.tsx` ruft jetzt den Server-PDF-Endpunkt auf statt `toPng()`. Dateiname: `.pdf` statt `.png`.
    69→  - Einzelne Chart-Downloads (Karte, Balken, Torte) bleiben als PNG via `html-to-image` — **kein Funktionsverlust**.
    70→  - PDF: A4 Querformat, automatische Seitenumbrüche.
    71→- **Nachträgliche Fixes (4 Iterationen):**
    72→  - **Fix 1 — Tabellen-Layout:** `doc.text()` verschob Y-Cursor automatisch bei jeder Spalte → `textAt()`-Helper mit `lineBreak: false` und manueller Y-Steuerung pro Zeile.
    73→  - **Fix 2 — Austria-Karte schwarz:** PDFKit versteht kein CSS `rgb()` → Hex-Format (`#rrggbb`). `.fill().stroke()` Chaining funktioniert nicht → `fillAndStroke()`.
    74→  - **Fix 3 — Kartentext-Kontrast:** Labels bei dunklem Heatmap-Hintergrund (z.B. Kärnten mit vielen Teilnehmern) unsichtbar → `getLabelColor()` berechnet wahrgenommene Helligkeit und wechselt zu weißem Text bei dunklem Hintergrund.
    75→  - **Fix 4 — A4-Layout-Optimierung:**
    76→    - **Alle Repeater angezeigt:** `slice(0, 6)` Limit entfernt, Multi-Row Grid (6 pro Zeile, automatisch umbrechen).
    77→    - **Charts auf BL-Ebene:** Balken-/Tortendiagramm nutzt immer Bundesland-Daten (max 9 Einträge) statt Bezirke (40+) für Lesbarkeit. Bezirk-Detail bleibt in der Tabelle.
    78→    - **Charts auf eigener Seite:** Kein Split durch `ensureSpace` mitten im Chart-Block.
    79→    - **Pie-Legend Safety:** Berechnet verfügbaren Platz, zeigt „… +N weitere" bei Überlauf.
    80→  - **Offen:** Spaltenheader bei Seitenumbrüchen in Bezirk-/Teilnehmer-Tabellen wiederholen.
    81→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `server/src/services/pdf.ts` (NEU), `server/src/routes/pdf.ts` (NEU), `server/src/index.ts`, `server/package.json`, `client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`, `client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`
    82→
    83→### #9 — Rapporte Verteilung schneidet Bundesland-Namen ab
    84→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/9
    85→- **Status:** Erledigt und deployed
    86→- **Problem:** Balken- und Tortendiagramm in der Webansicht (Auswertung) nutzten volle Bundesland-Namen (z.B. „Niederösterreich") als Labels — werden bei wenig Platz abgeschnitten.
    87→- **Fix:** Labels verwenden jetzt OE-Kurzform: `OE3` (Bundesland-Ebene) bzw. `OE3 AM` (Bezirk-Ebene mit Bezirkscode).
    88→- **Scope:** Nur Webansicht (Client). PDF-Labels waren bereits auf BL-Ebene.
    89→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`
    90→
    91→### #10 — Suffix-Checkboxen (/m /p /am /mm) statt Freitext im Rufzeichen
    92→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/10
    93→- **Status:** Erledigt
    94→- **Problem:** Operatoren gaben Suffixe wie `/m` (mobil), `/p` (portabel) direkt im Rufzeichen-Feld ein — fehleranfällig und uneinheitlich.
    95→- **Umsetzung:**
    96→  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `suffix TEXT DEFAULT NULL` in `signal_reports` (ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN, nicht-destruktiv).
    97→  - **API:** POST/PATCH/GET Reports akzeptieren und liefern `suffix`-Feld. Stats-Participants-Query nutzt `GROUP_CONCAT(DISTINCT sr.suffix)` als `suffixes`.
    98→  - **UI-Eingabe:** Toggle-Buttons (m, p, am, mm) neben dem Rufzeichen-Feld in LandMode und BundMode. Radio-Verhalten: nur einer aktiv, nochmal klicken = deselect. Blau bei aktiv, grau bei inaktiv.
    99→  - **Auswertung Web:** Teilnehmer-Tabelle zeigt Suffix neben Rufzeichen (z.B. "OE8YML/m").
   100→  - **Auswertung PDF:** Teilnehmer-Liste zeigt Callsign + Suffix.
   101→  - Bestehende Reports ohne Suffix funktionieren weiterhin (NULL = kein Suffix).
   102→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `schema.ts`, `exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts` (Routes), `pdf.ts` (Service), `stats.ts`, `LandMode.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`, `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`
   103→
   104→### #6 — Rufzeichen doppelt eingeben führt zu Problemen
   105→- **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/6
   106→- **Status:** Erledigt
   107→- **Problem:** Wenn ein Rufzeichen doppelt eingegeben wird (gleicher Operator + gleicher Repeater), greift die UNIQUE-Constraint der DB und der Server gibt 409 zurück. Der Client versuchte den existierenden Report per `reports.find()` im lokalen State zu finden, um ihn zu PATCHen. Bei veraltetem State passierte **gar nichts** — kein Feedback, kein Update.
   108→- **Fix:** Server liefert bei 409 den existierenden Report als `existing_report` im Response-Body mit. Client nutzt diesen direkt statt im lokalen State zu suchen. `apiFetch` hängt den vollen Response-Body als `error.data` am Error-Objekt an.
   109→- **Geänderte Dateien:** `exercises.ts` (Server), `api.ts`, `LandMode.tsx`, `BundMode.tsx`
   110→

GET liefert es automatisch mit `SELECT *`.\n  - **Stats-Queries:** Alle 4 Participants-Queries (exercises.ts, reports.ts, pdf.ts x2) selektieren `o.home_repeater`.\n  - **Stammdaten UI (OperatorsPage):** Neue Spalte \"Repeater\" in der Tabelle. Inline-Edit und Add-Form mit Freitext-Input.\n  - **Auswertung Web:** Neue Spalte \"Repeater\" in der Teilnehmer-Liste (ReportsPage + AggregatedReportsPage).\n  - **Auswertung PDF:** Neue Spalte \"REP.\" in der Teilnehmer-Liste zwischen Name und Bezirk.\n  - Operatoren ohne Repeater: Spalte bleibt leer.\n- **Geänderte Dateien:** `schema.ts`, `operators.ts`, `exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts` (Routes), `pdf.ts` (Service), `stats.ts`, `OperatorsPage.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`, `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`"

All changes implemented and build verified. Here's a summary of what was done:

**Issue #3: Eigener Repeater pro Operator (Stammdaten)**

### Changes made across 11 files:

1. **`server/src/db/schema.ts`** — Migration: `ALTER TABLE operators ADD COLUMN home_repeater TEXT DEFAULT NULL`

2. **`server/src/routes/operators.ts`** — POST accepts `home_repeater`, PATCH includes `home_repeater` in allowed fields

3. **`server/src/routes/exercises.ts`** — Stats participants query selects `o.home_repeater`

4. **`server/src/routes/reports.ts`** — Aggregated stats participants query selects `o.home_repeater`

5. **`server/src/routes/pdf.ts`** — Both participants queries (aggregated + single exercise) select `o.home_repeater`

6. **`server/src/services/pdf.ts`** — `Participant` interface updated, new "REP." column between Name and Bezirk with adjusted column widths

7. **`client/src/types/stats.ts`** — `Participant` interface: added `home_repeater: string | null`

8. **`client/src/pages/ReportsPage.tsx`** — New "Repeater" column in participants table

9. **`client/src/pages/AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`** — New "Repeater" column in participants table

10. **`client/src/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx`** — New "Repeater" column in table header, display row, inline edit, and add form

11. **`CHANGELOG-2026-02-24.md`** — Issue #3 documented as erledigt

 ## Offene Issues
 
-### #3 — Bei den Repeatern bitte „eigener Repeater" dazufügen — Klärung
+### #3 — Eigener Repeater pro Operator (Stammdaten)
 - **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/3
-- Klärungsbedarf: benutzerdefinierte Repeater können bereits über Einrichtung hinzugefügt werden. Zu klären, ob weitere Funktionalität gewünscht.
+- **Status:** Erledigt
+- **Umsetzung:**
+  - **Schema-Migration:** Neue Spalte `home_repeater TEXT DEFAULT NULL` in `operators`-Tabelle (Freitext, optional).
+  - **API:** POST/PATCH Operators akzeptieren `home_repeater`-Feld. GET liefert es automatisch mit `SELECT *`.
+  - **Stats-Queries:** Alle 4 Participants-Queries (exercises.ts, reports.ts, pdf.ts x2) selektieren `o.home_repeater`.
+  - **Stammdaten UI (OperatorsPage):** Neue Spalte "Repeater" in der Tabelle. Inline-Edit und Add-Form mit Freitext-Input.
+  - **Auswertung Web:** Neue Spalte "Repeater" in der Teilnehmer-Liste (ReportsPage + AggregatedReportsPage).
+  - **Auswertung PDF:** Neue Spalte "REP." in der Teilnehmer-Liste zwischen Name und Bezirk.
+  - Operatoren ohne Repeater: Spalte bleibt leer.
+- **Geänderte Dateien:** `schema.ts`, `operators.ts`, `exercises.ts`, `reports.ts`, `pdf.ts` (Routes), `pdf.ts` (Service), `stats.ts`, `OperatorsPage.tsx`, `ReportsPage.tsx`, `AggregatedReportsPage.tsx`
 
 ### #5 — Eventuell mehrere OPs bei Simplex
 - **Issue:** https://github.com/achildrenmile/bosarsalog/issues/5
